MA	<p>43a8_d1e8</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>PauI</i></span> is derived from Paracoccus alcaliphilus ZVK3-3, and <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>MfeI</i></span> is derived from Mycoplasma fermentans.
